# Forza Horizon 6 & Gran Turismo 7 — full car rosters

Retrieved: 2026-10-06

For a follow-up agent to download **one reference image per car**.
The `Image` column holds either a direct image URL (preferred) or, when no
verified per-car URL could be found, a short ready-to-use image-search query.

## Sources

| # | URL | Used for |
|---|-----|----------|
| 1 | https://forza.net/fh6cars | Official Forza Horizon 6 car list — 657 cars with Make / Name / Type / PI class / Country / Collection / add-on pack |
| 2 | https://forza.fandom.com/wiki/Category:Cars_(FH6) | Forza Wiki FH6 car pages (686), read through the MediaWiki API `action=query&prop=pageimages` for a direct render URL per car |
| 3 | https://gran-turismo.fandom.com/wiki/Gran_Turismo_7/Car_List | Gran Turismo Wiki GT7 car list — 583 cars as of v1.71, manufacturer grouping, per-car render URLs, `(DLC)` and `*` special-edition markers |
| 4 | https://gran-turismo.fandom.com/wiki/Gran_Turismo_7/Updates | GT7 update history (which version added which cars) |
| 5 | https://gtplus.app/gt7/cars | GT7 Plus database — credits price, Brand Central / Used / Legend availability, invite flag, post-launch update version |
| 6 | https://www.gran-turismo.com/sg/gt7/carlist | Official GT7 car list — “No. of Cars: 574 as of August 2026” |
| 7 | https://www.gran-turismo.com/gb/news/00_4305265.html | Official August 2026 update (v1.71) car additions |
| 8 | https://www.gran-turismo.com/gb/news/00_8895693.html | Official June 2026 update (v1.70) car additions |
| 9 | https://www.gran-turismo.com/gb/news/00_4842740.html | Official April 2026 update (v1.69) car additions + Power Pack DLC note |

## Forza Horizon 6

657 cars total: **610 base-roster cars** (grouped by manufacturer below) and **47 add-on / bonus cars** in a separate section. PI class is shown as `<class letter> <PI>` (the official list stores it the other way round). Every car has a direct Forza Wiki render URL.

## Gran Turismo 7

583 cars in this section: **574** from the GT7 Plus database (price + dealer) + **6** Power Pack DLC + **3** wiki-only entries. That matches the 583 cars the Gran Turismo Wiki lists for v1.71 (the official site still says 574 as of August 2026 — see Coverage).

`Source` is where the car is bought: Brand Central (new), Used Car Dealership, or Legend Cars. Notes carry the racing category, invite-only flag, the update that added the car, and special-edition markers.

## Coverage

### Forza Horizon 6

- **657 of 657 cars listed** (base 610 + add-on 47) — a complete dump of the official `forza.net/fh6cars` table, no rows skipped.
- **Images: 657/657 direct URLs** (Forza Wiki `pageimages` render for the matching `Category:Cars (FH6)` page). 0 cars fall back to a search query.
- 93 manufacturers in the full official table (92 have base-roster cars and appear below); the 47 add-on cars are split out of the manufacturer sections so the base roster can be downloaded first.
- *Gap:* the official list has no per-car image column, so image URLs come from the community Forza Wiki rather than an official press kit. Names were matched to wiki pages with a year-checked rule plus 9 hand-verified overrides (e.g. `1990 Eunos Cosmos` → `Eunos Cosmo`), so a URL may point at a wiki page whose title differs slightly from the official name.

### Gran Turismo 7

- **583 cars listed** = 574 (GT7 Plus DB, price + dealer) + 6 Power Pack DLC + 3 wiki-only = the 583 the Gran Turismo Wiki reports for v1.71.
- **Images: 583/583 direct URLs** — 575 from the GT7 Plus entries and 8 from the wiki-only/DLC rows; 0 fallbacks.
- Prices exist for **570 of 575** GT7 Plus entries; the 5 without are AFEELA 1 '26, AFEELA Prototype 2024, Mazda RX-VISION GT3 CONCEPT Stealth Model, Gran Turismo Red Bull X2019 25th Anniversary and Toyota Himedic '21.
- **Known discrepancy:** official `gran-turismo.com/gt7/carlist` says *574 cars as of August 2026*; the wiki says *583 (574 standard + 3 special editions + 6 paid DLC) as of v1.71*. This file follows the wiki count so nothing is missing, and marks the special/DLC cars individually.
- **Known gaps:**
  - The GT7 Plus DB has no record for 8 wiki cars, so those rows have no price/dealer (5 Power Pack DLC cars + `TOYOTA 86 GT"Limited" '16`, `Toyota GR Yaris 1st Edition RZ "High Performance" '20`, `Volkswagen Beetle 1966 Desert Racer`).
  - Dealer attribution is derived from flags (`legend` / `brandCentral` / `used`), not read off a per-car storefront field; 45 cars are flagged both Brand Central and Used and are reported as Brand Central.
  - Post-launch additions are marked from the DB's `update` field (152 cars carry one, v1.13–v1.71); cars that shipped at launch are unmarked, not explicitly labelled “launch”.

Machine-readable intermediate data for these tables: `reports/fh6_rows.json`, `reports/fh6_matched.json`, `reports/gt7_join.json`, `reports/gt7_fandom.json` (gitignored).
